Kamino gives curators granular controlover institutional-grade lending infrastructure.

[### Vaults](https://kamino.com/docs/curators/vaults)

1

### Deploy Vault

Initialize a lending vault that accepts a single deposit token.

2

### Set Allocation Strategy

Configure allocation weights, caps, and risk parameters across reserves.

3

### Route Assets

Distribute depositor assets across lending reserves and issue share tokens.

4

### Earn Revenue

Collect fees from vault operations and manage rewards distribution.

[### Markets](https://kamino.com/docs/curators/markets)

1

### Create Market

Deploy an isolated lending market with your own risk parameters.

2

### Add Reserves

Configure reserves with LTV, liquidation thresholds, rate curves, and oracles.

3

### Configure Rewards

Set up collateral and debt farms to distribute rewards to depositors and borrowers.

4

### Transfer to Multisig

Transfer market administration to a Squads multisig before launch.

A complete toolchain for faster integrations.
---------------------------------------------

#### REST API

Data, analytics, user positions, unsigned transactions.

#### TypeScript SDK

On-chain reads, transaction building, advanced operations.

#### Rust Crate

Rust clients, bots, on-chain CPI.

#### AI Tools

llms.txt, skill.md, agent assisted development.

#### CLI

Managing markets, reserves, vaults, and operational workflows.

    const res = await fetch('https://api.kamino.finance/ktx/kvault/deposit', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        wallet: 'YOUR_WALLET_ADDRESS',
        vault: 'HDsayqAsDWy3QvANGqh2yNraqcD8Fnjgh73Mhb3WRS5E',
        amount: '100.0',
      }),
    });
    
    const { transaction } = await res.json();
    

    import { createSolanaRpc, address, generateKeyPairSigner } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoVault } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    import { Decimal } from 'decimal.js';
    
    const signer = await generateKeyPairSigner();
    
    const vault = new KaminoVault(
      createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com'),
      address('HDsayqAsDWy3QvANGqh2yNraqcD8Fnjgh73Mhb3WRS5E')
    );
    
    const depositIxs = await vault.depositIxs(
      signer,
      new Decimal(100.0)
    );
    

    use kvault_interface::{helpers, pda, VaultInfo, KVAULT_PROGRAM_ID};
    use spl_associated_token_account::get_associated_token_address;
    
    let vault = VaultInfo::from_account_data(
        vault_pubkey, &vault_data.data, &reserve_infos
    )?;
    
    let user_token_ata = get_associated_token_address(&owner, &vault.token_mint);
    let (shares_mint, _) = pda::shares_mint(&KVAULT_PROGRAM_ID, &vault_pubkey);
    let user_shares_ata = get_associated_token_address(&owner, &shares_mint);
    
    let deposit_ix = helpers::deposit::deposit(
        &vault, owner, user_token_ata, user_shares_ata,
        1_000_000,
    );
    

#### Kamino MCP Server

`npx add-mcp [https://kamino.com/docs/mcp](https://kamino.com/docs/mcp) `

#### Kamino CLI

`yarn kamino-manager create-market —mode execute`

# Building with LLMs - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/building-with-llms#content-area)

Kamino documentation is optimized for AI-powered development. Our machine-readable resources enable LLMs and AI agents to understand our APIs and build transactions on Solana.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/building-with-llms#kamino-ai-resources)

Kamino AI Resources
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

skill.md
--------

Structured capability reference with API endpoints, SDK methods, and integration workflows. Start here for agent tool use.

llms.txt
--------

Curated index of all documentation pages with one-line descriptions and direct URLs.

llms-pages.json
---------------

Machine-readable JSON mapping of every page with title, section, URL, and keywords.

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/building-with-llms#skill-md)

skill.md

The skill.md file provides a comprehensive, machine-readable reference of Kamino’s capabilities across all four products: Earn, Borrow, Multiply, and Liquidity.

| Includes | Description |
| --- | --- |
| API endpoints | Complete REST endpoint reference for all products |
| SDK methods | TypeScript method signatures with real code examples |
| Workflows | Step-by-step operations: deposit, borrow, withdraw, repay, stake, harvest |
| Integration patterns | Earn vaults, Borrow markets, Liquidity strategies, and Farms |
| Use cases | Agent transaction building, API integration, automated DeFi strategies |

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/building-with-llms#llms-txt)

llms.txt

A structured navigation map of Kamino’s entire documentation, enabling LLMs to discover relevant pages and resources efficiently.

| Includes | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Page directory | All documentation pages with direct URLs |
| Tutorials | Organized by category: Earn, Borrow, Liquidity, Rewards, Oracle |
| API reference | Endpoint navigation and documentation links |
| Recipes | Code example locations and implementation guides |

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/building-with-llms#llms-pages-json)

llms-pages.json

A JSON file mapping every page path to its metadata: title, URL, section, description, and keywords. Useful for programmatic discovery and filtering.

    {
      "/products/lending-vaults": {
        "title": "Lending Vaults",
        "url": "https://kamino.com/docs/products/lending-vaults",
        "section": "Docs",
        "description": "Single-token managed yield aggregation across Kamino's lending markets",
        "keywords": ["kamino", "lending vaults", "yield", "vault shares", "auto-compounding"]
      }
    }
    

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

Get airdrop metrics

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.kamino.finance/v2/airdrop/metrics

    const options = {method: 'GET'};
    
    fetch('https://api.kamino.finance/v2/airdrop/metrics', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.kamino.finance/v2/airdrop/metrics"
    
    response = requests.get(url)
    
    print(response.text)

    false

200

400

404

500

marketPubkey

string

required

Kamino Lend market public key

Example:

`"7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/market-data/market-reserve-metrics-history#parameter-reserve-pubkey)

reservePubkey

string

required

Kamino Lend reserve public key

Example:

`"FBSyPnxtHKLBZ4UeeUyAnbtFuAmTHLtso9YtsqRDRWpM"`

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/market-data/market-reserve-metrics-history#parameter-env)

env

enum<string>

default:mainnet-beta

Solana cluster environment

Available options:

`mainnet-beta`,

`devnet`,

`localnet`

Example:

`"mainnet-beta"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/market-data/market-reserve-metrics-history#parameter-one-of-0)

start

stringnumberstringnumber

default:1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z

Date input (ISO 8601 string or epoch in ms)

Example:

`"2024-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/market-data/market-reserve-metrics-history#parameter-one-of-0)

end

stringnumberstringnumber

Date input (ISO 8601 string or epoch in ms)

Example:

`"2024-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/market-data/market-reserve-metrics-history#parameter-frequency)

frequency

enum<string>

default:hour

Frequency of the snapshots

Available options:

`hour`,

`day`

Example:

`"hour"`

#### Response

200

application/json

OK

      .then(res => res.json())

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.kamino.finance/v2/airdrop/users/{pubkey}/allocations"
    
    response = requests.get(url)

#### Path Parameters

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/airdrop/get-user-airdrop-allocations#parameter-pubkey)

pubkey

string

required

Valid base58-encoded address of the user wallet

Example:

`"AxqtG9SHDkZTLSWg81Sp7VqAzQpRqXtR9ziJ3VQAS8As"`

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/airdrop/get-user-airdrop-allocations#parameter-source)

source

string

Points source identifier

Examples:

`"Season1"`

`"Season2"`

`"Season3"`

`"Season4"`

#### Response

200

application/json

OK

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/airdrop/get-user-airdrop-allocations#response-items-quantity)

quantity

string

required

Quantity of tokens allocated to the user for the airdrop

Example:

`"1234.56789"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/airdrop/get-user-airdrop-allocations#response-items-name)

name

string

required

Name of the airdrop allocation source

Example:

`"main"`

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli#content-area)

The Kamino Manager CLI is a command line tool used to create and manage Kamino lending markets, reserves, and Kamino vaults. The Kamino CLI provides:

*   Create and configure lending markets with reserves for different token types and custom risk parameters
*   Build and manage automated liquidity vaults with adjustable allocation strategies and yield optimization
*   Monitor performance metrics and update market or vault settings as needed

Installation & Setup
--------------------

Install and configure the Kamino Manager CLI

Market Operations
-----------------

Create and manage Kamino lending markets and reserves

Vault Operations
----------------

Create and manage Kamino vaults

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

# Agent Integrations: Using OpenClaw with Kamino - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#content-area)

By the end of this tutorial, you will have the following skill files configured for your agent.

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| [`SKILL.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#skill-md) | Main skill instructions: capabilities, quick reference, and workflows |
| [`references/setup.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/setup-md) | Environment setup: RPC, wallet keypair, OpenClaw config |
| [`references/earn.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/earn-md) | Earn vault operations: deposit, withdraw, vault data, user positions |
| [`references/borrow.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/borrow-md) | Borrow market operations: deposit, borrow, repay, withdraw, market data |
| [`references/multiply.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/multiply-md) | Multiply operations: leveraged positions, flash loans, position health |
| [`references/liquidity.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/liquidity-md) | Liquidity strategy operations: deposit, withdraw, strategy metrics, user PnL |
| [`references/rewards.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/rewards-md) | Farm operations: stake, unstake, harvest, farm state, pending rewards |
| [`references/api.md`](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/api-md) | Complete REST API endpoint reference for all products |

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#prerequisites)

Prerequisites
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   Node.js 22+
*   Solana wallet with SOL for transaction fees
*   RPC endpoint

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#skill-folder-structure)

Skill Folder Structure
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1

Create Skill Directory

Create a new directory for the Kamino skill in your OpenClaw workspace:

    mkdir -p ~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/kamino
    cd ~/.openclaw/workspace/skills/kamino
    

2

Create File Structure

Create the following files in the directory:

    kamino/
    ├── SKILL.md              # Main skill instructions for the agent
    └── references/
        ├── setup.md          # Environment and SDK setup
        ├── earn.md           # Kamino Earn operations (vaults)
        ├── borrow.md         # Kamino Borrow operations (lending)
        ├── multiply.md       # Kamino Multiply operations (leverage)
        ├── liquidity.md      # Kamino Liquidity operations (strategies)
        ├── rewards.md        # Kamino Rewards operations (farms)
        └── api.md            # Kamino API reference
    

    mkdir -p references
    touch SKILL.md references/setup.md references/earn.md references/borrow.md references/multiply.md references/liquidity.md references/rewards.md references/api.md
    

3

Get a Solana RPC Endpoint

Choose an RPC provider:

*   **Free**: `https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com` (rate limited)
*   **Recommended**: Helius, QuickNode, or Triton (paid, production-ready)

4

Create or Use Existing Wallet Keypair

Create a new keypair if needed:

    solana-keygen new --outfile ~/solana-wallet.json
    

Or use an existing keypair file.

5

Configure OpenClaw

Add your Solana credentials to `~/.openclaw/openclaw.json`:

    {
      "gateway": {
        "env": {
          "SOLANA_RPC_URL": "https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com",
          "SOLANA_WS_URL": "wss://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com",
          "WALLET_KEYPAIR_PATH": "/path/to/your/solana-wallet.json"
        }
      }
    }
    

Replace `/path/to/your/solana-wallet.json` with the actual path to your keypair file.

6

Restart OpenClaw Gateway

After updating the configuration:

    openclaw gateway restart
    

7

Verify Setup

Test your RPC connection:

    curl -X POST $SOLANA_RPC_URL \
      -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
      -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"getHealth"}'
    

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#for-other-ai-agents)

For Other AI Agents

*   **Claude**: Copy the SKILL.md content into your conversation or attach as a file
*   **Cursor**: Clone into `.cursor/skills/kamino` directory
*   **Custom Agents**: Reference the markdown files directly

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#file-templates)

File Templates
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy the following templates into your skill files.

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#skill-md)

SKILL.md

The main skill file that teaches agents how to interact with Kamino:

    # Kamino Skill
    
    Interact with Kamino Finance on Solana: Earn vaults, Borrow markets, Liquidity strategies, and Rewards farms.
    
    ## Prerequisites
    
    See [setup.md](references/setup.md) for installation and configuration.
    
    Required:
    - Solana RPC endpoint
    - Wallet keypair for signing transactions
    - `@solana/kit` and relevant Kamino SDKs installed
    
    ## What You Can Do
    
    **Kamino Earn** - Deposit into yield-generating vaults
    **Kamino Borrow** - Supply collateral, borrow assets, repay loans
    **Kamino Multiply** - Open and manage leveraged positions
    **Kamino Liquidity** - Deposit into automated concentrated liquidity strategies
    **Kamino Rewards** - Stake, unstake, and harvest farm rewards
    **KSwap** - Swap between any two tokens via Kamino's swap aggregator
    **Oracle (Scope)** - Read token prices on-chain or via REST
    
    ## Quick Reference
    
    | Operation | Method | Reference |
    |-----------|--------|-----------|
    | Earn: Deposit | SDK or API | [earn.md](references/earn.md) |
    | Earn: Withdraw | SDK or API | [earn.md](references/earn.md) |
    | Borrow: Deposit Collateral | API | [borrow.md](references/borrow.md) |
    | Borrow: Borrow Assets | API | [borrow.md](references/borrow.md) |
    | Borrow: Repay Loan | API | [borrow.md](references/borrow.md) |
    | Borrow: Withdraw Collateral | API | [borrow.md](references/borrow.md) |
    | Multiply: Open Position | SDK | [multiply.md](references/multiply.md) |
    | Multiply: Monitor Health | SDK | [multiply.md](references/multiply.md) |
    | Multiply: Close Position | SDK | [multiply.md](references/multiply.md) |
    | Liquidity: Deposit | SDK | [liquidity.md](references/liquidity.md) |
    | Liquidity: Withdraw | SDK | [liquidity.md](references/liquidity.md) |
    | Liquidity: Strategy Data | SDK or API | [liquidity.md](references/liquidity.md) |
    | Rewards: Stake | SDK | [rewards.md](references/rewards.md) |
    | Rewards: Unstake | SDK | [rewards.md](references/rewards.md) |
    | Rewards: Harvest | SDK | [rewards.md](references/rewards.md) |
    | KSwap: Swap Tokens | API | [api.md](references/api.md) |
    | Oracle: Read Prices | SDK or API | [api.md](references/api.md) |
    | Query Market Data | SDK | [borrow.md](references/borrow.md) |
    | Get Vault/Market Lists | API | [api.md](references/api.md) |
    
    ## Core Workflow
    
    ### For Kamino Earn:
    1. Choose a vault (get list via API or use known address)
    2. Deposit tokens to earn yield
    3. Withdraw vault shares when needed
    
    ### For Kamino Borrow:
    1. Supply collateral to a market
    2. Borrow assets against your collateral
    3. Monitor health factor (keep LTV below 75%)
    4. Repay loans and withdraw collateral
    
    ### For Kamino Multiply:
    1. Choose a strategy (LST, JLP, stablecoin, or xStocks)
    2. Open a leveraged position with target leverage
    3. Monitor LTV and liquidation risk
    4. Adjust leverage, repay debt, or close position
    
    ### For Kamino Liquidity:
    1. Choose a strategy (get list via API or use known address)
    2. Deposit tokens (single-sided or two-sided)
    3. Monitor share price and PnL
    4. Withdraw shares when needed
    
    ### For Kamino Rewards:
    1. Find the farm for your position (by stake mint or known address)
    2. Stake share tokens into the farm
    3. Monitor pending rewards
    4. Harvest rewards or unstake and withdraw
    
    **SOL/BTC Market**: `7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF`
    
    ## Reference Files
    
    - **[setup.md](references/setup.md)** — Installation and OpenClaw configuration
    - **[earn.md](references/earn.md)** — Vault deposit/withdraw code examples
    - **[borrow.md](references/borrow.md)** — Lending market operations
    - **[multiply.md](references/multiply.md)** — Leveraged position operations
    - **[liquidity.md](references/liquidity.md)** — Liquidity strategy operations
    - **[rewards.md](references/rewards.md)** — Farm staking and reward harvesting
    - **[api.md](references/api.md)** — Complete API endpoint reference

    # Kamino Setup

    ## Prerequisites

    - Solana wallet keypair file for signing transactions

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#references/earn-md)

references/earn.md

Kamino Earn vault operations:

    # Kamino Earn Operations
    
    Deposit into yield-generating vaults, withdraw shares, and query vault/user data.
    
    ## Dependencies
    
    ```bash
    npm install @solana/kit @kamino-finance/klend-sdk decimal.js
    ```
    
    ## SDK Instantiation
    
    ```typescript
    import { createSolanaRpc, address } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoVault, KaminoManager } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    
    // Initialize a specific vault
    const vault = new KaminoVault(
      rpc,
      address('VAULT_PUBKEY_HERE')
    );
    
    // Or use manager for multi-vault operations
    const manager = new KaminoManager(rpc);
    ```
    
    ## SDK Operations
    
    | What to Do | SDK Method | Documentation |
    |------------|------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Deposit to vault | `vault.depositIxs(signer, amount)` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/earn/earn-via-sdk) |
    | Withdraw from vault | `vault.withdrawIxs(signer, shareAmount)` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/earn/earn-withdraw-via-sdk) |
    | **Read Vault Data** |
    | Get vault holdings | `vault.getVaultHoldings()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/vault-data) |
    | Get vault APY | `vault.getAPYs()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/vault-data) |
    | Get exchange rate | `vault.getExchangeRate()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/vault-data) |
    | Get allocations | `vault.getVaultAllocations()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/vault-data) |
    | Get vault overview | `manager.getVaultOverview(vault, tokenPrice)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/vault-data) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get user shares | `vault.getUserShares(userAddress)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get all positions | `manager.getUserSharesBalanceAllVaults(userAddress)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/earn/data/user-position-data) |
    
    ## API Operations
    
    | What to Do | API Endpoint | Documentation |
    |------------|--------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Deposit to vault | `POST /ktx/kvault/deposit` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/earn/earn-via-api) |
    | Withdraw from vault | `POST /ktx/kvault/withdraw` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/earn/earn-withdraw-via-api) |
    | **Read Vault Data** |
    | List all vaults | `GET /kvaults/vaults` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get vault details | `GET /vault/{vaultPubkey}` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get vault metrics | `GET /vault/{vaultPubkey}/metrics` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Historical metrics | `GET /vault/{vaultPubkey}/metrics/history` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get user positions | `GET /kvaults/users/{wallet}/positions` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get vault position | `GET /kvaults/users/{wallet}/vaults/{vaultPubkey}/position` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get position PnL | `GET /kvaults/users/{wallet}/vaults/{vaultPubkey}/pnl` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get transactions | `GET /kvaults/users/{wallet}/transactions` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - Withdrawals use **vault share amounts**, not underlying token amounts
    - SDK automatically handles farm unstaking if needed
    - API transactions use Address Lookup Tables - must use `decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables`
    - Historical data only available via API
    
    ## Complete Tutorial
    
    [Earn and Withdraw Tutorial](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/tutorials/earn/earn-and-withdraw)

references/borrow.md

Kamino Borrow operations (deposit collateral, borrow, repay, withdraw):

    # Kamino Borrow Operations
    
    Supply collateral, borrow assets, repay loans, withdraw collateral, and query market/user data.
    
    **SOL/BTC Market**: `7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF`
    
    ## Dependencies
    
    ```bash
    npm install @solana/kit @kamino-finance/klend-sdk decimal.js
    ```
    
    ## SDK Instantiation
    
    ```typescript
    import { createSolanaRpc, address } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoMarket, VanillaObligation, PROGRAM_ID } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const marketPubkey = address('7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF');
    
    // Load market with 400ms slot duration
    const market = await KaminoMarket.load(rpc, marketPubkey, 400, PROGRAM_ID);
    
    // Get user's obligation
    const userAddress = address('USER_WALLET_ADDRESS');
    const obligation = await market.getObligationByWallet(
      userAddress,
      new VanillaObligation(PROGRAM_ID)
    );
    ```
    
    ## SDK Operations
    
    | What to Do | SDK Method | Documentation |
    |------------|------------|---------------|
    | **Read Market Data** |
    | Get total deposit TVL | `market.getTotalDepositTVL()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get total borrow TVL | `market.getTotalBorrowTVL()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get all reserves | `market.getReserves()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get reserve deposit TVL | `reserve.getDepositTvl()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get reserve borrow TVL | `reserve.getBorrowTvl()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get reserve borrow APY | `reserve.totalBorrowAPY(slot)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get reserve supply APY | `reserve.totalSupplyAPY(slot)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | Get reserve utilization | `reserve.calculateUtilizationRatio()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/market-data) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get user's obligation | `market.getObligationByWallet(user, obligationType)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get all user obligations | `market.getAllUserObligations(user)` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get LTV ratio | `obligation.loanToValue()` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get borrow utilization | `obligation.refreshedStats.borrowUtilization` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get user deposits | `obligation.deposits` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    | Get user borrows | `obligation.borrows` | [Data Docs](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/borrow/data/user-position-data) |
    
    ## API Operations
    
    | What to Do | API Endpoint | Documentation |
    |------------|--------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Deposit collateral | `POST /ktx/klend/deposit` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/borrow/deposit-via-api) |
    | Borrow assets | `POST /ktx/klend/borrow` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/borrow/borrow-via-api) |
    | Repay loan | `POST /ktx/klend/repay` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/borrow/repay-via-api) |
    | Withdraw collateral | `POST /ktx/klend/withdraw` | [Recipe](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/recipes/borrow/withdraw-via-api) |
    | **Read Market Data** |
    | List all markets | `GET /v2/kamino-market` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get market details | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Historical reserve APY | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/reserves/{reservePubkey}/metrics/history` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get user obligations | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/users/{userPubkey}/obligations` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get obligation details | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/obligations/{obligationPubkey}` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get obligation PnL | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/users/{userPubkey}/obligations/{obligationPubkey}/pnl` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    | Get user rewards | `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/users/{userPubkey}/rewards` | [API Docs](https://api-docs.kamino.finance) |
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - **Address Lookup Tables**: All API transactions use ALTs, must use `decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables`
    - **Health checks**: Always verify obligation LTV before borrowing or withdrawing collateral
    - **Commitment levels**: Use `finalized` for blockhash, `confirmed` for transaction sends
    - **Error handling**: Wrap all operations in try-catch blocks
    - **Historical data**: Only available via API
    
    ## Complete Tutorials
    
    - [Deposit and Borrow Tutorial](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/tutorials/borrow/deposit-and-borrow)
    - [Repay and Withdraw Tutorial](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/tutorials/borrow/repay-and-withdraw)

references/multiply.md

Kamino Multiply leveraged position operations:

    # Kamino Multiply Operations
    
    Open leveraged positions, monitor health, adjust leverage, and close positions.
    
    ## Dependencies
    
    ```bash
    npm install @solana/kit @kamino-finance/klend-sdk @kamino-finance/kswap-sdk @kamino-finance/scope-sdk decimal.js
    ```
    
    ## SDK Instantiation
    
    ```typescript
    import { createSolanaRpc, address } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoMarket, PROGRAM_ID, getFlashLoanInstructions, getDepositWithLeverageIxs, getRepayWithCollIxs } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const marketPubkey = address('7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF');
    
    const market = await KaminoMarket.load(rpc, marketPubkey, 400, PROGRAM_ID);
    ```
    
    ## SDK Operations
    
    | What to Do | SDK Method | Documentation |
    |------------|------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Open leveraged position | `getDepositWithLeverageIxs({...})` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/operations/deposit-xstocks) |
    | Repay with collateral | `getRepayWithCollIxs({...})` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/operations/repay-collateral) |
    | Flash loan | `getFlashLoanInstructions({...})` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/flash-loans) |
    | **Read Position Data** |
    | Get multiply positions | `market.getUserObligationsByTag(ObligationTypeTag.Multiply, userAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/data/health-metrics) |
    | Get position LTV | `obligation.refreshedStats.loanToValue` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/data/health-metrics) |
    | Get liquidation LTV | `obligation.refreshedStats.liquidationLtv` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/data/health-metrics) |
    | Get net value | `obligation.refreshedStats.netAccountValue` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/data/health-metrics) |
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - Multiply uses flash loans to open positions atomically in a single transaction
    - Leveraged combo operations (`getDepositWithLeverageIxs`, `getRepayWithCollIxs`) are TypeScript SDK only, not available in Rust
    - Always monitor LTV to avoid liquidation
    - Requires KSwap SDK for swap routing and Scope SDK for oracle price refresh
    - Fetch pair LUTs from `cdn.kamino.finance/resources.json` before building transactions
    
    ## Complete Guides
    
    - [Flash Loans](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/flash-loans)
    - [Open Position (xStocks)](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/operations/deposit-xstocks)
    - [Repay with Collateral](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/operations/repay-collateral)
    - [Monitor Position Health](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/multiply/data/health-metrics)

references/liquidity.md

Kamino Liquidity strategy operations:

    # Kamino Liquidity Operations
    
    Deposit into automated concentrated liquidity strategies, withdraw shares, and query strategy/user data.
    
    ## Dependencies
    
    ```bash
    npm install @solana/kit @kamino-finance/kliquidity-sdk decimal.js
    ```
    
    ## SDK Instantiation
    
    ```typescript
    import { createSolanaRpc, address } from '@solana/kit';
    import { Kamino } from '@kamino-finance/kliquidity-sdk';
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const kamino = new Kamino('mainnet-beta', rpc);
    ```
    
    ## SDK Operations
    
    | What to Do | SDK Method | Documentation |
    |------------|------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Single-sided deposit | `kamino.singleSidedDepositTokenB(strategy, amount, signer, slippageBps)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/operations/deposit) |
    | Two-sided deposit | `kamino.deposit(strategyWithAddress, amountA, amountB, signer)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/operations/deposit) |
    | Withdraw all shares | `kamino.withdrawAllShares(strategyWithAddress, signer)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/operations/withdraw) |
    | **Read Strategy Data** |
    | Get all strategies | `kamino.getAllStrategiesWithFilters(filters)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/data/strategy) |
    | Get share price and balances | `kamino.getStrategyShareData(strategyAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/data/strategy) |
    | Get share price only | `kamino.getStrategySharePrice(strategyAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/data/strategy) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get user positions | `kamino.getUserPositions(walletAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/data/user-position) |
    
    ## API Operations
    
    | What to Do | API Endpoint | Documentation |
    |------------|--------------|---------------|
    | **Read Strategy Data** |
    | List all strategies | `GET /strategies?status=LIVE` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/strategy-data/strategies-list) |
    | Get strategy metrics | `GET /strategies/{pubkey}/metrics` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/strategy-data/strategy-metrics) |
    | Historical metrics | `GET /v2/strategies/{pubkey}/history` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/strategy-data/strategy-metrics-history) |
    | Total TVL | `GET /strategies/tvl` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/strategy-data/strategies-tvl) |
    | Fees and rewards | `GET /strategies/fees-and-rewards?period=24h` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/strategy-data/strategies-fees-and-rewards) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | User PnL | `GET /v2/strategies/{pubkey}/shareholders/{wallet}/pnl` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/user-data/user-pnl) |
    | User transactions | `GET /v2/shareholders/{wallet}/transactions` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/liquidity/user-data/user-transactions) |
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - Single-sided deposit auto-swaps half the input via the SDK's built-in swap router
    - Withdrawals return both Token A and Token B proportionally
    - Use `POST /luts/find-minimal` for address lookup table resolution on deposits and withdrawals
    - Historical data only available via API
    
    ## Complete Guide
    
    - [Deposit Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/operations/deposit)
    - [Withdraw Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/liquidity/operations/withdraw)

references/rewards.md

Kamino Rewards farm operations:

    # Kamino Rewards Operations
    
    Stake into farms, unstake, harvest rewards, and query farm/user data.
    
    ## Dependencies
    
    ```bash
    npm install @solana/kit @kamino-finance/farms-sdk decimal.js
    ```
    
    ## SDK Instantiation
    
    ```typescript
    import { createSolanaRpc, address } from '@solana/kit';
    import { Farms, FarmState } from '@kamino-finance/farms-sdk';
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const farms = new Farms(rpc);
    ```
    
    ## SDK Operations
    
    | What to Do | SDK Method | Documentation |
    |------------|------------|---------------|
    | **Transactions** |
    | Stake tokens | `farms.stakeIx(...)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/stake) |
    | Unstake tokens | `farms.unstakeIx(...)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/unstake) |
    | Withdraw unstaked | `farms.withdrawUnstakedDepositIx(...)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/withdraw-unstaked) |
    | Harvest all rewards | `farms.claimForUserForFarmAllRewardsIx(...)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/harvest) |
    | **Read Farm Data** |
    | Get farm state | `FarmState.fetch(rpc, farmAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/farm-state) |
    | Batch-load farms | `farms.getAllFarmStatesByPubkeys(addresses)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/farm-state) |
    | Find farms by mint | `farms.getFarmsForMint(stakeMint)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/farm-state) |
    | Get incentive APY | `farms.getRewardsAPYForStrategy(strategyAddress)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/farm-state) |
    | **Read User Data** |
    | Get all user positions | `farms.getAllFarmsForUser(walletAddress, now)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/user-position) |
    | Get pending rewards | `farms.getUserPendingRewards(userState, farmState, now, oraclePrices)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/user-position) |
    | Get lockup status | `farms.getLockupDurationAndExpiry(farm, user, now)` | [Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/user-position) |
    
    ## API Operations
    
    | What to Do | API Endpoint | Documentation |
    |------------|--------------|---------------|
    | Farm transactions | `GET /farms/{farmPubkey}/transactions` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/rewards/farm-transactions) |
    | Farm management history | `GET /farms/{farmPubkey}/management-history` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/rewards/farm-management-history) |
    | User farm transactions | `GET /farms/users/{pubkey}/transactions` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/rewards/user-transactions) |
    | Oracle prices (for reward valuation) | `GET /oracles/prices` | [API Ref](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/oracle/oracle-prices) |
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - **Delegated farms** (klend reserve farms, kvault farms) have no stake mint; a delegate authority stakes on behalf of users
    - **Non-delegated farms** own their stake token; use `getFarmsForMint` to discover them
    - Unstaking triggers a **withdrawal cooldown** before tokens can be withdrawn
    - Reward valuation requires oracle prices from `GET /oracles/prices?source=scope`
    - Farm operations are SDK-only; there are no transaction-building API endpoints for farms
    
    ## Complete Guide
    
    - [Farm State Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/farm-state)
    - [User Position Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/data/user-position)
    - [Stake Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/stake)
    - [Harvest Guide](https://docs.kamino.finance/build/developers/rewards/operations/harvest)

references/api.md

Kamino REST API endpoints:

    # Kamino API Reference
    
    Quick reference for Kamino Finance REST API.
    
    ## Base URL
    
    `https://api.kamino.finance`
    
    No API key required for public endpoints.
    
    ## Complete Documentation
    
    - Full API Reference: https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference
    - OpenAPI Specification: https://api-docs.kamino.finance
    
    ## Key Endpoints
    
    **Kamino Earn:**
    - `POST /ktx/kvault/deposit` - Deposit to vault
    - `POST /ktx/kvault/withdraw` - Withdraw from vault
    - `GET /kvaults/vaults` - List all vaults
    - `GET /kvaults/users/{walletAddress}/positions` - User positions
    
    **Kamino Borrow:**
    - `POST /ktx/klend/deposit` - Deposit collateral
    - `POST /ktx/klend/borrow` - Borrow assets
    - `POST /ktx/klend/repay` - Repay loan
    - `POST /ktx/klend/withdraw` - Withdraw collateral
    - `GET /v2/kamino-market` - List all markets
    - `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/users/{userPubkey}/obligations` - User obligations
    
    **Kamino Multiply:**
    - Multiply operations are SDK-only (no API transaction endpoints)
    - Monitor positions via `GET /kamino-market/{marketPubkey}/users/{userPubkey}/obligations`
    
    **Kamino Liquidity:**
    - `GET /strategies?status=LIVE` - List all strategies
    - `GET /strategies/{pubkey}/metrics` - Strategy metrics
    - `GET /v2/strategies/{pubkey}/history` - Historical metrics
    - `GET /strategies/tvl` - Total TVL
    - `GET /v2/strategies/{pubkey}/shareholders/{wallet}/pnl` - User PnL
    - `GET /v2/shareholders/{wallet}/transactions` - User transactions
    
    **Kamino Rewards (Farms):**
    - `GET /farms/{farmPubkey}/transactions` - Farm transactions
    - `GET /farms/{farmPubkey}/management-history` - Farm management history
    - `GET /farms/users/{pubkey}/transactions` - User farm transactions
    
    **Oracle:**
    - `GET /oracles/prices` - Oracle prices with timestamp and staleness
    - `GET /prices` - Token USD prices by mint
    
    **KSwap (Token Swaps):**
    - `GET /kswap/swap/` - Get best route and transaction for swapping between two tokens
    
    **Utilities:**
    - `POST /luts/find-minimal` - Find minimal Address Lookup Tables
    
    ## Important Notes
    
    - All transaction endpoints return base64-encoded transactions
    - Transactions use Address Lookup Tables and require `decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables`
    - Client must decode, sign with fresh blockhash, and submit transactions
    - Farm operations (stake, unstake, harvest) are SDK-only with no API transaction endpoints

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/agentic-finance/openclaw-agent-integration#usage-examples)

Usage Examples
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Once installed, your agent can execute commands like:

*   “Deposit 100 USDC into the SOL-USDC vault”
*   “Check my current lending positions on Kamino”
*   “Withdraw 50% of my vault shares”
*   “Supply 1000 USDC to the lending market”
*   “What’s the APY on the SOL-USDC vault?”
*   “Deposit into the KMNO/USDC liquidity strategy”
*   “What are my pending farm rewards?”
*   “Harvest all rewards from my staked positions”
*   “Swap 1 SOL to USDC”
*   “Get the current SOL price from the oracle”

For detailed code examples, see the Kamino documentation links in the reference files.

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

marketPubkey

string

required

Kamino Lend market public key

Example:

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/user-and-loans-data/obligation-pnl#parameter-obligation-pubkey)

obligationPubkey

string

required

Kamino Lend obligation public key

Example:

`"63QrAB1okxCc4FpsgcKYHjYTp1ua8ch6mLReyKRdc22o"`

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/user-and-loans-data/obligation-pnl#parameter-env)

env

enum<string>

default:mainnet-beta

Solana cluster environment

Available options:

`mainnet-beta`,

`devnet`,

`localnet`

Example:

`"mainnet-beta"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/borrow/user-and-loans-data/obligation-pnl#parameter-program-id)

programId

string

default:KLend2g3cP87fffoy8q1mQqGKjrxjC8boSyAYavgmjD

useStakeRate

enum<string>

default:false

For xSOL pairs, calculate the PnL using the stake rate

Available options:

`true`,

`false`

Example:

`"true"`

200

application/json

OK

string

required

string

required

object

boolean

# Claim Rewards - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#content-area)

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#claiming-rewards)

Claiming Rewards
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Retrieve and claim all pending rewards from a vault position.

1

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#import-dependencies)

Import Dependencies

Import the required packages for Solana RPC communication, Kamino SDK operations, and Kit transaction building.

    import {
      createSolanaRpc,
      createSolanaRpcSubscriptions,
      address,
      pipe,
      createTransactionMessage,
      setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner,
      setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash,
      appendTransactionMessageInstructions,
      signTransactionMessageWithSigners,
      sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory,
      getSignatureFromTransaction,
    } from '@solana/kit';
    import {
      KaminoManager,
      KaminoVault,
      getMedianSlotDurationInMsFromLastEpochs,
      parseKeypairFile,
    } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    

2

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#load-signer-and-initialize-rpc)

Load Signer and Initialize RPC

Load your keypair from a file and initialize RPC connections for both standard and subscription-based operations.

    const KEYPAIR_FILE = '/path/to/your/keypair.json';
    const signer = await parseKeypairFile(KEYPAIR_FILE);
    
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const rpcSubscriptions = createSolanaRpcSubscriptions('wss://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    

Replace `/path/to/your/keypair.json` with the actual path to your keypair file. The `rpcSubscriptions` enables real-time transaction confirmation.

3

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#initialize-manager-and-vault)

Initialize Manager and Vault

Initialize the Kamino manager with slot timing and create a vault instance.

    const vaultAddress = address('HDsayqAsDWy3QvANGqh2yNraqcD8Fnjgh73Mhb3WRS5E'); // USDC vault
    
    const slotDuration = await getMedianSlotDurationInMsFromLastEpochs();
    const kaminoManager = new KaminoManager(rpc, slotDuration);
    const vault = new KaminoVault(rpc, vaultAddress);
    

The `getMedianSlotDurationInMsFromLastEpochs` function calculates the median slot duration from recent epochs for accurate timing. This vault instance represents a specific Kamino vault you want to claim rewards from.

4

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#generate-claim-rewards-instructions)

Generate Claim Rewards Instructions

Request all claim reward instructions for the vault from the Kamino manager.

    const claimRewardsIxs = await kaminoManager.getClaimAllRewardsForVaultIxs(signer, vault);
    
    if (!claimRewardsIxs.length) {
      throw new Error('No instructions returned by Kamino SDK');
    }
    

The `getClaimAllRewardsForVaultIxs` method generates instructions to claim all available rewards for your position in the specified vault. If no instructions are returned, there may be no claimable rewards available.

5

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#build-and-sign-transaction)

Build and Sign Transaction

Use Kit’s functional pipe pattern to build and sign the transaction with a fresh blockhash.

    const { value: latestBlockhash } = await rpc.getLatestBlockhash().send();
    
    const transactionMessage = pipe(
      createTransactionMessage({ version: 0 }),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner(signer, tx),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash(latestBlockhash, tx),
      (tx) => appendTransactionMessageInstructions(claimRewardsIxs, tx)
    );
    
    const signedTransaction = await signTransactionMessageWithSigners(transactionMessage);
    

Kit’s `pipe` function enables functional composition of transaction building steps. The signed transaction is ready to be sent to the network.

6

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/tutorials/claim-rewards#send-and-confirm-transaction)

Send and Confirm Transaction

Send the transaction to the network and wait for confirmation using the subscription-based confirmation method.

    const signature = getSignatureFromTransaction(signedTransaction);
    
    await sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory({ rpc, rpcSubscriptions })(signedTransaction, {
      commitment: 'confirmed',
      skipPreflight: true,
    });
    
    console.log('Claim rewards successful! Signature:', signature);
    

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

---

# Kamino Portfolio API - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio#content-area)

The Portfolio API returns a wallet’s positions across **every Kamino product in one call**: lending, multiply, leverage, liquidity, earn, private credit, and staking. It’s the single request a dashboard makes to paint the whole picture, plus a companion endpoint for pending farm rewards.

**Source data, composed by you.** Positions arrive already valued (each carries `value`/`price`), but portfolio-wide totals, net worth, and PnL are intentionally left to client composition, so you build exactly the rollups your app needs. See [Calculate portfolio totals](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio/response#calculate-portfolio-totals)
.

**No API key required.** All Portfolio endpoints are public on `https://api.kamino.finance`.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio#endpoints)

Endpoints
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

| Method | Endpoint | Returns |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET` | `/portfolio/{pubkey}` | All positions across the seven product sections |
| `GET` | `/portfolio/{pubkey}/rewards` | Pending farm rewards across all products |

See the full field reference in [Portfolio response](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio/response)
.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio#what-you-can-build)

What you can build
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Portfolio dashboards:** render every position a wallet holds across Kamino from one request.
*   **Liquidation and health monitors:** read `ltv` against `liquidationLtv` on lending, multiply, and leverage positions to alert before liquidation.
*   **Net-worth and PnL tools:** sum `netValue` across sections and convert to USD with the [Oracle prices](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/oracle/data/prices)
     endpoint.
*   **Reward trackers:** surface pending farm rewards across lending, borrowing, and liquidity in one view.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio#quickstart)

Quickstart
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Fetch a wallet’s full portfolio and count positions per product.

    const WALLET = 'YOUR_WALLET_ADDRESS'; // replace with your wallet pubkey
    const API = 'https://api.kamino.finance';
    
    const portfolio = await (await fetch(`${API}/portfolio/${WALLET}`)).json();
    
    console.log('Timestamp:', portfolio.timestamp);
    
    const products = ['lending', 'multiply', 'leverage', 'liquidity', 'earn', 'privateCredit', 'staking'] as const;
    products.forEach((p) => console.log(`  ${p}: ${(portfolio[p] || []).length} position(s)`));
    

Numeric fields are returned as **strings** (for example `"0.38"`, `"100.05"`). Parse them with `parseFloat` before doing math.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/portfolio#next-steps)

Next steps
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Portfolio response
------------------

Full field reference for every product section, plus runnable examples.

API reference
-------------

The raw OpenAPI spec for both Portfolio endpoints.

Oracle prices
-------------

Convert position amounts to USD when composing totals.

Rewards
-------

Read and aggregate pending farm rewards across products.

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

---

# Claim User Rewards - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#content-area)

**Delegated vs Non-Delegated Farms**Different farm types use different delegation patterns. For the correct delegatees for your specific farm, check the user farm state returned by `getAllFarmsForUser` in the “Calculate Claimable User Rewards” tutorial.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#claim-user-rewards)

Claim User Rewards
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Claim all pending rewards from a user’s farm position.

1

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#import-dependencies)

Import Dependencies

Import the required packages for Solana RPC communication, Kamino SDK operations, and Kit transaction building.

    import {
      createSolanaRpc,
      createSolanaRpcSubscriptions,
      address,
      pipe,
      createTransactionMessage,
      setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner,
      setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash,
      appendTransactionMessageInstructions,
      signTransactionMessageWithSigners,
      sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory,
      getSignatureFromTransaction,
    } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoMarket, parseKeypairFile, VanillaObligation, PROGRAM_ID } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    import { Farms } from '@kamino-finance/farms-sdk';
    

2

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#load-signer-and-initialize-rpc)

Load Signer and Initialize RPC

Load your keypair from a file and initialize RPC connections.

    // Configuration - UPDATE THESE VALUES
    const KEYPAIR_FILE = '/path/to/your/keypair.json';
    
    // Load keypair from file
    const signer = await parseKeypairFile(KEYPAIR_FILE);
    
    // Initialize RPC and RPC Subscriptions
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const rpcSubscriptions = createSolanaRpcSubscriptions('wss://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    

3

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#load-market-and-get-obligation)

Load Market and Get Obligation

Load the Kamino market and get the user’s obligation address.

    const marketPubkey = address('7u3HeHxYDLhnCoErrtycNokbQYbWGzLs6JSDqGAv5PfF');
    const market = await KaminoMarket.load(rpc, marketPubkey, 400);
    
    // Get obligation address for delegatees array
    const kaminoObligation = await market!.getObligationByWallet(signer.address, new VanillaObligation(PROGRAM_ID));
    if (!kaminoObligation) {
      throw new Error('No obligation found');
    }
    
    console.log('Obligation:', kaminoObligation.obligationAddress.toString());
    

4

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#build-claim-instructions)

Build Claim Instructions

Use the Farms SDK to build claim instructions for all rewards on the farm.

    // Build claim instructions using farms-sdk
    const farm = new Farms(rpc);
    const claimIxs = await farm.claimForUserForFarmAllRewardsIx(
      signer, // payer
      signer.address, // user (wallet address = owner)
      address('Gsoc83qRQ5X1LfGGbPqU2FdxZnveqbkP2vMXq6BcmtMk'), // farm
      true, // isDelegated = true for lending farms
      [signer.address, kaminoObligation.obligationAddress] // delegatees array
    );
    

The `delegatees` array values depend on your specific farm position. To find the correct delegatee addresses for your farm, use the “Calculate Claimable User Rewards” tutorial - it shows the actual delegatee in the user farm state.

5

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#build-and-sign-transaction)

Build and Sign Transaction

Use Kit’s functional pipe pattern to build and sign the transaction with a fresh blockhash.

    const { value: latestBlockhash } = await rpc.getLatestBlockhash({ commitment: 'finalized' }).send();
    const claimTx = pipe(
      createTransactionMessage({ version: 0 }),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner(signer, tx),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash(latestBlockhash, tx),
      (tx) => appendTransactionMessageInstructions(claimIxs, tx)
    );
    const claimSigned = await signTransactionMessageWithSigners(claimTx);
    const claimSignature = getSignatureFromTransaction(claimSigned);
    

6

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/developers/borrow/rewards/claim-user-rewards#send-and-confirm-transaction)

Send and Confirm Transaction

Send the transaction to the network and wait for confirmation.

    await sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory({ rpc, rpcSubscriptions })(claimSigned, {
      commitment: 'confirmed',
      skipPreflight: true,
    });
    
    console.log(`Claim farm rewards successful! Signature: ${claimSignature}`);
    

The rewards have been claimed and transferred to your wallet. You can verify the transaction using the signature on a Solana explorer.

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

---

# Install and Setup the Kamino CLI - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#content-area)

The Kamino Manager CLI is a command-line tool for creating and managing Kamino lending markets, reserves, and vaults.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#prerequisites)

Prerequisites
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Before installing the Kamino Manager CLI:

Node.js, Yarn package manager, and Solana CLI tools are required to run the Kamino Manager CLI.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#installation)

Installation
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Follow these steps to install and configure the Kamino Manager CLI:

1

Install Node.js and Yarn

Install Node.js from [nodejs.org](https://nodejs.org/)
, then install Yarn package manager.

    npm install -g yarn
    

2

Install Solana CLI

Install the Solana CLI tools to create and manage keypairs.

    sh -c "$(curl -sSfL https://release.solana.com/v1.18.18/install)"
    

After installation, restart the terminal to ensure the Solana CLI is available in PATH.

3

Clone and Setup Kamino Manager

Clone the Kamino SDK repository and install dependencies.

    git clone git@github.com:Kamino-Finance/klend-sdk.git
    cd klend-sdk
    yarn
    

This will install all necessary dependencies for the Kamino Manager CLI.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#configuration)

Configuration
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

After installation, configure the Kamino Manager CLI with a wallet and RPC endpoint.

1

Create Admin Keypair

Create a new keypair that will serve as the admin account for managing markets and vaults.

    solana-keygen new -o admin.json
    

Store the keypair file securely and never share it. This keypair will need SOL for transaction fees.

Existing keypairs can be used instead of creating a new one. Specify the path to the existing keypair file in the configuration.

2

Configure Environment Variables

Create a `.env` file in the `klend-sdk` directory and configure the following variables:

    # Path to admin keypair file
    ADMIN=./admin.json
    
    # Solana RPC endpoint
    RPC=https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com
    
    # Program IDs
    KLEND_PROGRAM_ID_MAINNET="KLend2g3cP87fffoy8q1mQqGKjrxjC8boSyAYavgmjD"
    KVAULT_PROGRAM_ID_MAINNET="KvauGMspG5k6rtzrqqn7WNn3oZdyKqLKwK2XWQ8FLjd"
    KLEND_PROGRAM_ID_STAGING="SLendK7ySfcEzyaFqy93gDnD3RtrpXJcnRwb6zFHJSh"
    KVAULT_PROGRAM_ID_STAGING="stKvQfwRsQiKnLtMNVLHKS3exFJmZFsgfzBPWHECUYK"
    KVAULT_PROGRAM_ID_DEVNET="devkRngFnfp4gBc5a3LsadgbQKdPo8MSZ4prFiNSVmY"
    

For production use, it’s recommended to use a private RPC endpoint from providers like [Helius](https://www.helius.dev/)
. Public RPCs are rate-limited and may cause transaction failures.

3

Fund Your Admin Account

Transfer SOL to the admin account to pay for transaction fees. Get the admin account address:

    solana-keygen pubkey admin.json
    

Transfer SOL to this address from another wallet or acquire it from an exchange.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#verify-installation)

Verify Installation
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Run a simple command to verify everything is set up correctly:

    yarn run kamino-manager --help
    

The help output should display available commands for managing markets and vaults.

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/cli/installation-setup#next-steps)

Next Steps
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Kamino Manager CLI is now installed and ready for managing markets and vaults:

Market Operations
-----------------

Create and manage lending markets and reserves

Vault Operations
----------------

Create and manage Kamino vaults

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

source

200

application/json

rewardToken

lastCalculated

string<date>

required

Timestamp of the last calculation

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/earn/vault-data/vaults-rewards#response-items-apy)

apy

string

required

Annual Percentage Yield from rewards in the kvault

Example:

`"0.2"`

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/api-reference/earn/vault-data/vaults-rewards#response-items-tokens-per-second)

tokensPerSecond

string

required

Number of reward tokens distributed per second

Example:

`"1234.56789"`

# Borrow Overview - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index

![](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/kamino-dots.svg?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=c22a1a90c3c51266bda1cdaebf0bf90c)

Borrow &  
Lend
===============

Integrate borrow and lending markets, and build leveraged positions with Multiply.

$2.7B

Market Size

$1B

Active Borrows

18+

Audits

How It Works
------------

A high-level view of the Borrow model.

![Deposit icon](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/deposit.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=53bc161b4c626170aaa69beb8e71c5ac)

### Deposit

User supplies collateral tokens. An obligation is created to track their position and enable borrowing capacity.

![Borrow icon](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/borrow.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=072f03b7dd49734f5e8ed91e8b1e2ba2)

### Borrow

User borrows assets against collateral. LTV ratio determines borrow limit. Interest accrues on borrowed amount.

![Repay icon](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/repay.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=508a361e80b59603a4e6eea9e1a3ff65)

### Repay

User repays borrowed assets plus interest. Reduces debt, improves health factor, and unlocks collateral for withdrawal.

Borrow Integration Model
------------------------

Built for seamless integration. Borrow centers on three core layers of composability.

[Markets & Reserves](https://kamino.com/docs/build/borrow#bim-markets)
[Transactions](https://kamino.com/docs/build/borrow#bim-transactions)
[Positions & Health](https://kamino.com/docs/build/borrow#bim-positions)

![Layer 1, Markets and Reserves: browse reserves to supply and borrow capital and understand each market's conditions before routing users](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/integration-markets.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=371f7f35af253e90b53e8e60239b0e07)

![Layer 2, Transactions: turn lending actions into signable flows, from account setup to obligation management and transaction construction](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/integration-transactions.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=134293e9afb525e017cc5f0a8165943d)

![Layer 3, Positions and Health: bring account health into focus across the borrow lifecycle to keep users within healthy borrowing ranges](https://mintcdn.com/kamino-3d73a151/wADNksI7oget4gqs/images/borrow/integration-positions.png?w=2500&fit=max&auto=format&n=wADNksI7oget4gqs&q=85&s=47cac655686e3385370bfec350d91775)

### Markets & Reserves

Browse reserves to supply and borrow capital, and understand the conditions of each market before routing users.

Best Tools: REST API, TypeScript SDK

Available Market Data: supply APY, borrow APY, utilization, liquidity, market configuration, reserve metadata, reward farms, and bonus yield.

### Transactions

Turn lending actions into signable flows, from account setup to obligation management and transaction construction.

Best Tools: TypeScript SDK, REST API, Rust Interface Crate

Available Transaction Flows: user account setup, obligation account creation, transaction construction, flash loans.

### Positions & Health

Bring account health into focus across the borrow lifecycle, helping users stay within healthy borrowing ranges.

Best Tools: TypeScript SDK, REST API

Available Position Data: obligations, user PnL, portfolio value, collateral balances, debt balances, LTV, health factors, borrowing capacity, liquidation thresholds, and position history.

# Get Vault APYs - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#content-area)

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#how-vault-apy-is-calculated)

How Vault APY Is Calculated
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Vault APY is a blended rate based on the reserves the vault’s capital is deployed across.

    Vault APY ≈ Σ (Reserve APY × Allocation Weight)
    

Each reserve earns yield based on its utilization and borrow rate. A reserve at 80% utilization earning 8% APY contributes to the vault’s total yield based on its allocation weight, the share of capital deployed there. Allocation weights sum to 100% across all active reserves. **Example — a USDC vault with two allocations:**

| Reserve | Allocation | APY |
| --- | --- | --- |
| USDC SOL/BTC Market | 60% | 8%  |
| USDC JLP Market | 40% | 12% |

    Blended APY = (60% × 8%) + (40% × 12%) = 9.6%
    

Underlying borrow rates and utilization levels change continuously, so vault APY fluctuates. Quoted APYs are trailing averages and are not guaranteed forward-looking rates. The displayed APY includes base interest from borrowers plus KMNO emissions when active. APY accounts for compounding. In Kamino vaults, earnings are continuously reinvested, increasing the vault share price over time.

*   API
    
*   SDK
    

1

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#fetch-all-vaults)

Fetch All Vaults

Retrieve the list of all available vaults on Kamino.

    const API_BASE_URL = 'https://api.kamino.finance';
    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/kvaults/vaults`);
    const vaults = await response.json();
    

The `/kvaults/vaults` endpoint returns all vaults with basic information including vault addresses, token mints, and strategies.

2

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#get-apy-for-each-vault)

Get APY for Each Vault

Fetch APY metrics for all vaults in parallel using the vault public keys.

    // Fetch all vault metrics in parallel
    const vaultMetrics = await Promise.all(
      vaults.map(async (vault) => {
        const response = await fetch(
          `${API_BASE_URL}/kvaults/vaults/${vault.address}/metrics`
        );
        const metrics = await response.json();
    
        return {
          address: vault.address,
          apy7d: metrics.apy7d,
          apy30d: metrics.apy30d,
          apy90d: metrics.apy90d,
        };
      })
    );
    
    // Display all vault APYs
    vaultMetrics.forEach((vault) => {
      console.log(`\n${vault.address}:`);
      console.log(`  7-day APY: ${(vault.apy7d * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
      console.log(`  30-day APY: ${(vault.apy30d * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
      console.log(`  90-day APY: ${(vault.apy90d * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
    });
    

The `/kvaults/vaults/{pubkey}/metrics` endpoint returns historical APY data across multiple timeframes (7-day, 24-hour, 30-day, 90-day, 180-day, 365-day).

You now have APY data for all vaults across multiple timeframes.

1

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#import-dependencies)

Import Dependencies

Import the required packages for Solana RPC communication and Kamino SDK.

    import { createSolanaRpc } from '@solana/kit';
    import { KaminoManager, KaminoVault } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk';
    

2

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#initialize-sdk)

Initialize SDK

Set up the RPC connection and create a Kamino Manager instance.

    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const kaminoManager = new KaminoManager(rpc, 400);
    

3

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#get-all-vaults)

Get All Vaults

Retrieve all available vaults using the Kamino Manager.

    const vaults = await kaminoManager.getAllVaults();
    

4

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/get-vault-apys#get-apys-for-each-vault)

Get APYs for Each Vault

Fetch APY data for all vaults in parallel.

    // Fetch APYs for all vaults in parallel
    const vaultAPYs = await Promise.all(
      vaults.map(async (vaultData) => {
        const vault = new KaminoVault(rpc, vaultData.address);
        const apys = await vault.getAPYs();
    
        return {
          address: vaultData.address.toString(),
          name: vaultData.state?.name,
          actualAPY: apys.actualAPY.netAPY,
          theoreticalAPY: apys.theoreticalAPY.netAPY,
        };
      })
    );
    
    // Display all vault APYs
    vaultAPYs.forEach((vault) => {
      console.log(`\n${vault.address}:`);
      console.log(`  Actual APY: ${vault.actualAPY}`);
      console.log(`  Theoretical APY: ${vault.theoreticalAPY}`);
    });
    

The `vault.getAPYs()` method returns `theoreticalAPY` and `actualAPY`, each containing `grossAPY` and `netAPY` (after fees) calculated from on-chain state.

You’ve successfully fetched vault APYs using the Kamino SDK.

# Earn Deposit with the API - Kamino Docs

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/docs/llms.txt](https://kamino.com/docs/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#content-area)

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#earn-deposit-with-api)

Earn Deposit with API
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#import-dependencies)

Import Dependencies

Import the required packages for Solana RPC communication, transaction handling, and Kamino SDK utilities.

    import {
      createSolanaRpc,
      createSolanaRpcSubscriptions,
      pipe,
      getTransactionDecoder,
      getCompiledTransactionMessageDecoder,
      decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables,
      setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner,
      setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash,
      addSignersToTransactionMessage,
      signTransactionMessageWithSigners,
      sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory,
      getSignatureFromTransaction,
    } from '@solana/kit';
    import { parseKeypairFile } from '@kamino-finance/klend-sdk/dist/utils/signer.js';
    

2

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#configure-and-initialize)

Configure and Initialize

Set up the configuration and initialize RPC connections.

    const KEYPAIR_FILE = '/path/to/your/keypair.json';
    const API_BASE_URL = 'https://api.kamino.finance';
    
    const signer = await parseKeypairFile(KEYPAIR_FILE);
    const rpc = createSolanaRpc('https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    const rpcSubscriptions = createSolanaRpcSubscriptions('wss://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com');
    

3

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#call-kamino-api)

Call Kamino API

Make a POST request to the Kamino API to build the deposit transaction.

    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/ktx/kvault/deposit`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        wallet: signer.address,
        kvault: 'HDsayqAsDWy3QvANGqh2yNraqcD8Fnjgh73Mhb3WRS5E',
        amount: '1.0',
      }),
    });
    
    if (!response.ok) {
      const error = await response.json();
      throw new Error(`API error: ${error.message}`);
    }
    
    const { transaction: encodedTransaction } = await response.json();
    

The API returns a pre-built transaction encoded in base64 format. This transaction includes Address Lookup Tables (ALTs) for efficiency.

4

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#decode-transaction)

Decode Transaction

Decode the base64-encoded transaction and extract the message bytes.

    const txBuffer = Buffer.from(encodedTransaction, 'base64');
    const txMessageBytes = getTransactionDecoder().decode(txBuffer).messageBytes;
    
    const compiledMessage = getCompiledTransactionMessageDecoder().decode(txMessageBytes);
    

5

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#resolve-address-lookup-tables)

Resolve Address Lookup Tables

Fetch and resolve the Address Lookup Tables from the blockchain.

    const { value: latestBlockhash } = await rpc.getLatestBlockhash({ commitment: 'finalized' }).send();
    
    const signedTransaction = await pipe(
      await decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables(compiledMessage, rpc),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageLifetimeUsingBlockhash(latestBlockhash, tx),
      (tx) => setTransactionMessageFeePayerSigner(signer, tx),
      (tx) => addSignersToTransactionMessage([signer], tx),
      (tx) => signTransactionMessageWithSigners(tx)
    );
    

`decompileTransactionMessageFetchingLookupTables` fetches the lookup table data from the blockchain and resolves all address references. This is required for transactions that use ALTs.

A fresh blockhash is fetched and set to ensure the transaction remains valid.

6

### 

[​](https://kamino.com/docs/build/earn/recipes/earn-via-api#send-and-confirm-transaction)

Send and Confirm Transaction

Submit the signed transaction to the Solana network.

    const signature = getSignatureFromTransaction(signedTransaction);
    
    console.log(`Sending transaction with signature: ${signature}`);
    
    await sendAndConfirmTransactionFactory({ rpc, rpcSubscriptions })(signedTransaction, {
      commitment: 'confirmed',
      skipPreflight: true,
    });
    
    console.log('Deposit successful! Signature:', signature);
    

Your deposit is complete! The API built the transaction, you signed it locally (keeping your keys secure), and it was successfully submitted to the network.

Assistant

Responses are generated using AI and may contain mistakes.

---

Call Kamino API

Make a POST request to the Kamino API to build the withdrawal transaction.

    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/ktx/kvault/withdraw`, {

      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
